| 2026-11-20 01:30 UTC | 2026-11-20 03:30 UTC | Football - Brazilian League | Brasileirao Seria A is the first Brazilian league founded in 1959. Due to geographical reasons, the history of the single league in Brazil is relatively short. Only with the development of civil aviation did Brazil get a unique football league. There are currently 20 teams participating in the league in a two-round competition system. During its existence, Serie A has given the soccer world some of the best players in history.
